// FUNC 18003f73c __scrt_dllmain_after_initialize_c
char _scrt_dllmain_after_initialize_c()
{
  _crt_argv_mode startup_argv_mode; // eax

  if ( (unsigned int)_scrt_is_ucrt_dll_in_use() != 0 )
  {
    _isa_available_init();
  }
  else
  {
    startup_argv_mode = get_startup_argv_mode();
    if ( configure_narrow_argv(startup_argv_mode) != 0 )
      return 0;
    initialize_narrow_environment();
  }
  return 1;
}

{

    return 0;
  if ( !_acrt_thread_attach() )
  {
    _vcrt_thread_detach();
    return 0;
  }
  return 1;
}
// ENDFUNC

  return 1;
}

// FUNC 18003f828 __scrt_dllmain_uninitialize_c
void _scrt_dllmain_uninitialize_c()
{
  if ( (unsigned int)_scrt_is_ucrt_dll_in_use() != 0 )
  {
    execute_onexit_table(&module_local_atexit_table);
  }
  else if ( is_c_termination_complete() == 0 )
  {
    cexit();
  }
}
// ENDFUNC

}

// FUNC 18003f86c __scrt_initialize_crt
char __fastcall _scrt_initialize_crt(int a1)
{
  if ( a1 == 0 )
    is_initialized_as_dll = true;
  _isa_available_init();
  if ( !_acrt_initialize() )
    return 0;
  if ( !_acrt_initialize() )
  {
    _vcrt_uninitialize(false);
    return 0;
  }
  return 1;
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 18003f8a8 __scrt_initialize_onexit_tables

{

// FUNC 180043844 capture_current_context
struct _IMAGE_RUNTIME_FUNCTION_ENTRY *__fastcall capture_current_context(PCONTEXT ContextRecord)
{

  struct _IMAGE_RUNTIME_FUNCTION_ENTRY *result; // rax
  unsigned __int64 ImageBase; // [rsp+50h] [rbp+8h] BYREF
  unsigned __int64 EstablisherFrame; // [rsp+58h] [rbp+10h] BYREF
  PVOID HandlerData; // [rsp+60h] [rbp+18h] BYREF

  RtlCaptureContext(ContextRecord);
  Rip = ContextRecord->Rip;
  result = RtlLookupFunctionEntry(Rip, &ImageBase, nullptr);
  if ( result != nullptr )
    return (struct _IMAGE_RUNTIME_FUNCTION_ENTRY *)RtlVirtualUnwind(
                                                     0,
                                                     ImageBase,

                                                     &EstablisherFrame,
                                                     nullptr);

}
